## Current Best X-HD Status

- Goal5186: author `hd_exec` on full public Stanford Dragon/HappyBuddha matches
  the paper-branch author-log HDResult within `1e-6`.
- Goal5187: RTDL all-source route on the same public pair matches author
  HDResult with abs diff about `2.38e-9`.
- Goal5188: phase matrix records author internal timing, author process wall,
  RTDL route, and RTDL total separately and explicitly refuses a performance
  ratio.
- Goal5189: local-grid-cell seed route still matches author HDResult and lowers
  same-POD route wall from about `8.31s` to about `5.98s`, but increases
  frontier rows and continuation work.
- Goal5190: grid-branch-bound seed also matches author HDResult and tightens
  frontier rows to about `1.81M`, but route wall is about `7.71s`; it is an
  optional strategy/control, not the current best route.
- Goal5191: increasing generic native inline-nearest to
  `max_inline_points=512` consumes all frontier rows on the full public
  Dragon/HappyBuddha Level-B route; the empty-frontier passthrough route still
  matches author HDResult and records route wall about `3.65s`.
- Goal5192: optional native inline-nearest telemetry shows that the Goal5191
  route performs about `1.24B` inline point-distance evaluations inside native
  OptiX payload code. The telemetry run is diagnostic and slightly slower; the
  best performance route remains the no-telemetry inline512 route.
- Goal5193: a generic bounded grid-cell seed and intermediate inline thresholds
  were tested. They matched author HDResult but did not improve the current
  best route. The default remains local-grid-cell plus inline512.
- Goal5194: payload-current-best pruning in the native inline-nearest any-hit
  path matched author HDResult, lowered warmed route wall to about `3.46s`, and
  reduced inline point evaluations from about `1.24B` to `0.40B`.
- Goal5195: intersection-stage current-best pruning in the native inline-nearest
  route matched author HDResult, lowered warmed route wall to about `2.6s`, and
  lowered native frontier / inline time to about `0.93-0.94s`.
- Goal5196: dense grid-cell position lookup in the generic local-grid seed
  matched author HDResult, lowered route wall to about `2.26s`, and lowered seed
  time to about `0.55s`. Dense lookup was also applied to grid-cell-budget and
  grid-branch-bound controls; they matched but did not beat dense local-grid.
- Goal5197: native intersection now reports `min_sq` to any-hit via OptiX
  attributes, and row-only min/max distances are computed lazily only for row
  emission. It matched author HDResult and remains about `2.25-2.28s`; this is
  generic cleanup / neutral optimization, not a new speedup headline.
- Goal5198: grid-shape telemetry shows 32^3 remains the current default among
  tested shapes. 24^3 fails the empty-frontier route at capacity 0; 48^3/64^3/
  128^3 match but are slower even though they reduce inline point evaluations.
- Goal5199: trace-tmax-bound telemetry tested a generic OptiX ray extent
  tightening. It matched author HDResult but did not change inline cell hits or
  point evaluations and did not improve route wall, so the temporary code
  change was reverted. The default route remains unchanged.
- Goal5200: an explicit native CUDA executor for the generic local-grid-cell
  seed matched author HDResult and passed focused POD tests, but was slower
  than the existing auto/Numba seed on the same POD (`2.436s` route wall vs
  `2.258s`; seed `0.958s` vs `0.563s`). The default remains auto/Numba; the
  native CUDA seed is an experimental flag and no-go evidence, not a performance
  improvement.
- Goal5201: diagnostic native phase timing for the generic cell-MBR frontier
  collector matched author HDResult and shows `frontier_rows ~= 0.920s`,
  native frontier total `~= 0.600s`, native OptiX launch / inline-nearest work
  `~= 0.377s`, and native `accel_build ~= 0.0004s`. Prepared accel-build caching
  is not the next target.
- Goal5202: generic packed coordinate matrix reuse matched author HDResult,
  reports all seed/frontier coordinate-matrix reuse flags true, and improves
  the no-timing full-public route wall to about `2.027s` versus the Goal5200
  auto/Numba control at about `2.258s`.
- Goal5203: app-owned NumPy matrix input loader matched author HDResult and
  improves the no-timing full-public route wall to about `1.238-1.239s`.
  Source+target column construction is now about `0.001-0.002s`, so do not
  reopen tuple-row/matrix-repack work without new evidence.
- Goal5204: generic linear max-nearest reduction matched author HDResult and
  improves the route wall to about `1.17-1.18s`. Max-nearest reduction is now
  about `0.0007-0.0008s`, so do not reopen full-array reducer lexsort without
  new evidence.
- Goal5205: app-owned fast ASCII PLY matrix loader matched author HDResult and
  reduces public `load_full_inputs` from about `1.69s` to about `0.68s`.
  Full gate total drops to about `2.06s`, while route wall remains about
  `1.16-1.17s`. Do not reopen PLY per-line parsing unless new input evidence
  appears.
- Goal5206: first-use vs same-process warm diagnostic matched author HDResult
  and shows one-shot route remains about `1.16-1.17s`, while a near-identical
  second case in the same process is about `0.61s`. This is diagnostic only:
  warm numbers must not replace the fresh headline without an explicit prepared
  runtime regime and preparation-cost accounting.
- Goal5207: explicit route warmup protocol matched author HDResult. Warmup
  `all` is recorded separately (`case_total ~=1.389s`) and measured `all`
  reports route `~=0.626s`, with total including load + warmup + measured
  `~=2.893s`. Warmup is excluded from measured summary statistics.
- Goal5208: lowering `max_inline_points` below 512 matched author HDResult but
  did not improve the route. 384 is noise-level and full-run worse; 256/128 are
  slower because frontier row materialization and continuation dominate. Keep
  inline512 as the current default.
- Goal5209: static generic cell primitive ordering was tested. `point-count-asc`
  matched but moved repeated route median by only about `1.4ms`, not enough to
  change the default; `point-count-desc` was slower. Keep native cell order.
- Goal5210: the generic cell-MBR frontier OptiX raygen now uses
  `OPTIX_RAY_FLAG_DISABLE_CLOSESTHIT`, which is semantically appropriate for a
  pipeline with no closest-hit program, but same-POD repeats show no material
  route or OptiX launch speedup. Treat it as neutral semantic cleanup, not a
  solved native inline-nearest floor.
- Goal5211: optional generic global-bound early break for max-nearest /
  directed-Hausdorff reductions matched the Goal5186 author HDResult and
  materially improved the route. Fresh no-warm route is about `0.849s`, full
  total including load is about `1.752s`, and explicit-warm measured route
  median is about `0.362s`. About `409k` sources early-abort. This is review
  pending and must remain explicit until reviewed; early-aborted per-source
  witnesses may be approximate.
- Goal5212: all-source no-copy selection in the full-public runner matched the
  Goal5186 author HDResult and removes the `select_source_subset ~=0.216s`
  all-source copy. Fresh full total including load is now about `1.531s` with
  Goal5211 enabled; explicit-warm measured case total is about `0.288s`.
- Exact paper dataset identity remains unproved.
- Full paper reproduction remains unclosed.

## Immediate Implementation Target

Post-Goal5221: do not broaden ModelNet40 again until the single failing
`range_hood` comparator/regime mismatch is understood.

Recommended next goal:

```text
Goal5222: investigate range_hood_0124.off -> range_hood_0004.off.
  paper log HDResult = 0.46497631072998047
  current author XHD rerun = 0.466653436422348
  RTDL normalized route = 0.46497629417671404

Determine whether the paper-branch Algorithm=Hybrid comparator can be rebuilt
or emulated, whether another current author flag combination reproduces the
paper log, or whether this must be carried as a known author-rerun regime
mismatch while RTDL/paper-log value still matches.
```

```text
range_hood_hybrid_comparator_reproduced
range_hood_current_author_rerun_mismatch_classified_keep_modelnet40_partial
```

Do not claim ModelNet40 full reproduction, exact paper dataset identity, or an
author-vs-RTDL performance ratio until the batch gate passes and a separate
review authorizes the stronger status.

Post-Goal5212: send Goals5211-5212 for strict review, then decide whether to
make the global-bound early-break flag the X-HD Level-B route default for the
directed-HD/max-nearest use case. Do not make it default for generic
nearest-witness APIs.

Goal5211 implemented the optional generic global-bound early-break experiment
for the native cell-MBR inline-nearest route.

The last three local/micro attempts were no-go or neutral:

```text
Goal5208: lower inline thresholds did not improve the route;
Goal5209: static point-count cell ordering did not improve the route;
Goal5210: disabling closest-hit is semantic cleanup with no material speedup.
```

Goal5211 is different because it changes the execution model: it gives the
traversal path access to global max-nearest / directed-Hausdorff reduction state
so completed queries can raise a global bound and later queries can abort once
they cannot increase that bound.

Observed current best full-public Level-B route is dense-lookup local-grid plus
inline-nearest threshold 512 plus payload-current-best pruning plus
intersection-stage current-best pruning plus intersection attribute `min_sq`
reuse / lazy row distance plus empty-frontier passthrough plus packed
coordinate-matrix reuse plus NumPy matrix input loading plus fast ASCII PLY
matrix parsing:

```text
frontier_rows = 0
nearest_continuation ~= 0.016s
initial_state_seed ~= 0.23s
frontier_rows ~= 0.74s
source+target columns ~= 0.001-0.002s
max_nearest_reduction ~= 0.001s
rtdl_route_wall ~= 1.17-1.18s
same-process warm route_wall ~= 0.61s (diagnostic only)
explicit warmup measured route_wall ~= 0.626s
explicit warmup case_total ~= 1.389s
load_full_inputs ~= 0.681-0.682s
full gate total ~= 2.06s
```


Constraints:

- keep the work generic RTDL system work;
- no X-HD-specific primitive or author-specific shortcut;
- preserve tie-break semantics: lower MBR distance, lower cell id, lower item id;
- use the Goal5187/Goal5189 all-source author match as the correctness gate;
- keep performance claims route-local unless a denominator review authorizes
  broader comparison.

Candidate directions:

1. Review Goal5211 specifically for correctness of the max-nearest global-bound
   contract and the approximate-witness boundary.
2. Review Goals5191-5211 as a batch before turning any route-local number into
   release language; many are implemented / review pending.
3. If continuing performance after Goal5211, target a larger generic native
   traversal/indexing change or the inline point-scan execution model, not Python continuation,
   simple grid-shape tuning, or scalar ray `tmax` tuning. Goal5198 shows finer
   grids reduce point evals but lose to seed/cell-hit overhead on this workload;
   Goal5199 shows trace extent tightening does not change the hit/eval set;
   Goal5200 shows a host-to-native CUDA seed wrapper is slower unless the
   surrounding target/grid state becomes device-resident; Goal5201 shows
   prepared cell-MBR accel build is not material; Goal5203 shows tuple-row
   repacking is removed; Goal5204 shows max-reducer full lexsort is removed;
   Goal5205 shows ASCII PLY per-line parsing is no longer the dominant
   user-visible front-door issue; Goal5206 shows seed executor flag swapping
   does not fix one-shot cost and warm numbers require explicit regime labels.
   Do not spend the next goal on those solved costs.
4. If pursuing the warm runtime line beyond Goal5207, design a generic
   prepared/warm API rather than only an app-owned measurement flag. Continue to
   report preparation cost plus warm route cost side by side. Do not silently
   use the `~0.61s` / `~0.626s` warm route as the default result.
5. If no generic route attack is justified, pause optimization and move to
   review/provenance/figure planning rather than inventing an X-HD-specific
   shortcut.

Goal5211 acceptance checklist:

```text
- app-neutral API and metadata names;
- optional flag, default off until evidence justifies default;
- early-aborted source witness rows explicitly marked approximate;
- HDResult still matches Goal5186 author value;
- fresh and explicit-warm regimes kept separate;
- no author-vs-RTDL ratio;
- no X-HD-specific native/core symbol.
```

  next major goal.
